pointer.y - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n\n        if (pointer.active && distSq < mouseInfluenceRadiusSq && distSq > 4) {\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n          const force = (1 - dist / mouseInfluenceRadius);\n\n          if (config.mode === 'attract') {\n            // 引き寄せ\n            const pull = force * 0.6 * config.speed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull;\n          } else if (config.mode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            const push = force * 1.8 * config.speed;\n            p.vx -= (dx / dist) * push;\n            p.vy -= (dy / dist) * push;\n          } else if (config.mode === 'orbit') {\n            // 公転運動（法線ベクトル方向）\n            const normalX = -dy / dist;\n            const normalY = dx / dist;\n            const orbitSpeed = force * 1.5 * config.speed;\n            p.vx += normalX * orbitSpeed + (dx / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n            p.vy += normalY * orbitSpeed + (dy / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n          } else if (config.mode === 'trail') {\n            // 軌跡として追従\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force * 1.2 * config.speed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force * 1.2 * config.speed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 自然な空気抵抗\n        p.vx *= 0.96;\n        p.vy *= 0.96;\n\n        // 最低速度の維持（完全に止まらないように）\n        const speedSq = p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy;\n        if (speedSq < 0.04) {\n          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n          p.vx += Math.cos(angle) * 0.25 * config.speed;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.25 * config.speed;\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限\n        const maxVelocity = 7.5 * config.speed;\n        if (speedSq > maxVelocity * maxVelocity) {\n          const currentSpeed = Math.sqrt(speedSq);\n          p.vx = (p.vx / currentSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / currentSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx;\n        p.y += p.vy;\n\n        // 壁バウンド\n        if (p.x < 0) {\n          p.x = 0;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        } else if (p.x > width) {\n          p.x = width;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        }\n        if (p.y < 0) {\n          p.y = 0;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        } else if (p.y > height) {\n          p.y = height;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        }\n\n        // 呼吸パルス（サイズゆらぎ）\n        p.life += 0.035;\n        p.radius = p.baseRadius * (1 + Math.sin(p.life) * 0.28);\n\n        // 描画\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, p.radius), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n\n        if (config.glowEnabled) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 8;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        } else {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル間の結合線描画\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p1 = particlesRef.current[i];\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < particlesRef.current.length; j++) {\n          const p2 = particlesRef.current[j];\n          const cdx = p1.x - p2.x;\n          const cdy = p1.y - p2.y;\n          const distSq = cdx * cdx + cdy * cdy;\n\n          if (distSq < connectionDistSq) {\n            const opacity = (1 - distSq / connectionDistSq) * 0.38;\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);\n            ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n            ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;\n            ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n            ctx.stroke();\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // バースト（花火・クリック時）パーティクル処理\n      for (let b = burstsRef.current.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {\n        const bp = burstsRef.current[b];\n        bp.x += bp.vx;\n        bp.y += bp.vy;\n        bp.vx *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy += 0.08; // わずかな重力\n        bp.alpha -= bp.decay;\n\n        if (bp.alpha <= 0) {\n          burstsRef.current.splice(b, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(bp.x, bp.y, Math.max(0.5, bp.size * bp.alpha), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = bp.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, bp.alpha);\n\n        if (config.glowEnabled) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 10;\n          ctx.shadowColor = bp.color;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n      animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      isRunning = false;\n      if (animFrameIdRef.current) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);\n      }\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    };\n  }, [config, onUpdateStats, createParticle]);\n\n  // ポインターイベントリスナー\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n\n    pointerRef.current = { x, y, active: true };\n    addBurst(x, y, 40);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    pointerRef.current.x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    pointerRef.current.y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n    pointerRef.current.active = true;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    // 指を離しても位置は保ちつつ弱める\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n    pointerRef.current.x = -9999;\n    pointerRef.current.y = -9999;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"canvas-container\">\n      <canvas\n        ref={canvasRef}\n        className=\"particle-canvas\"\n        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n        onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}\n        onPointerLeave={handlePointerLeave}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ParticleCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

[config.theme]);\n\n  // パーティクル1粒を初期化\n  const createParticle = useCallback(\n    (width: number, height: number): Particle => {\n      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const speed = (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.8) * config.speed;\n      const baseRadius = (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.9) * config.size;\n\n      return {\n        x: Math.random() * width,\n        y: Math.random() * height,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        baseRadius,\n        radius: baseRadius,\n        color: getRandomThemeColor(),\n        alpha: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.6,\n        life: Math.random() * 100,\n        maxLife: 150 + Math.random() * 100,\n      };\n    },\n    [config.speed, config.size, getRandomThemeColor]\n  );\n\n  // 花火・バーストエフェクトの発生\n  const addBurst = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number, burstAmount = 45) => {\n      const newBursts: BurstParticle[] = [];\n      const palette = THEME_PALETTES[config.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < burstAmount; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / burstAmount + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const velocity = 2.5 + Math.random() * 6.5;\n        const color = palette.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.colors.length)];\n\n        newBursts.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocity,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocity,\n          color,\n          alpha: 1.0,\n          size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.5,\n          decay: 0.012 + Math.random() * 0.018,\n        });\n      }\n      burstsRef.current.push(...newBursts);\n    },\n    [config.theme]\n  );\n\n  // パーティクル総数の調整\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n config.count - currentLength; i++) {\n        particlesRef.current.push(createParticle(width, height));\n      }\n    } else if (currentLength > config.count) {\n      particlesRef.current.splice(config.count);\n    }\n  }, [config.count, createParticle]);\n\n  // テーマ変更時に色を再割り当て\n  useEffect(() => {\n    particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n      p.color = = canvasRef.current;\n      if (canvas) {\n        const cx = canvas.width / 2;\n        const cy = canvas.height / 2;\n        addBurst(cx, cy, 70);\n      }\n      prevTriggerBurstCount.current = triggerBurstCount;\n    }\n  }, [triggerBurstCount, addBurst]);\n\n  // アニメーションループ & 描画\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = || 1;\n      const displayWidth = canvas.clientWidth;\n      const displayHeight = canvas.clientHeight;\n\n      if (canvas.width !== displayWidth || canvas.height !== displayHeight) {\n - lastTimeRef.current >= 600) {\n        const fps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - lastTimeRef.current));\n        onUpdateStats?.(fps, particlesRef.current.length + burstsRef.current.length);\n        lastTimeRef.current = time;\n        frameCountRef.current = 0;\n      }\n\n      // 背景クリア（残像を残すために半透明黒でフェード）\n      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6, 8, 14, 0.28)';\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      const pointer = pointerRef.current;\n      const connectionDistSq = config.connectionDistance * config.connectionDistance;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadius = 220;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadiusSq = mouseInfluenceRadius * mouseInfluenceRadius;\n\n      // パーティクル物理演算\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // ポインターとの距離\n        const dx = pointer.x - p.x;\n        const dy = pointer.y - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n\n        if (pointer.active && distSq < mouseInfluenceRadiusSq && distSq > 4) {\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n          const force = (1 - dist / mouseInfluenceRadius);\n\n          if (config.mode === 'attract') {\n            // 引き寄せ\n            const pull = force * 0.6 * config.speed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull;\n          } else if (config.mode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            const push = force * 1.8 * config.speed;\n            p.vx -= (dx / dist) * push;\n            p.vy -= (dy / dist) * push;\n          } else if (config.mode === 'orbit') {\n            // 公転運動（法線ベクトル方向）\n            const normalX = -dy / dist;\n            const normalY = dx / dist;\n            const orbitSpeed = force * 1.5 * config.speed;\n            p.vx += normalX * orbitSpeed + (dx / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n            p.vy += normalY * orbitSpeed + (dy / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n          } else if (config.mode === 'trail') {\n            // 軌跡として追従\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force * 1.2 * config.speed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force * 1.2 * config.speed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 自然な空気抵抗\n        p.vx *= 0.96;\n        p.vy *= 0.96;\n\n        // 最低速度の維持（完全に止まらないように）\n        const speedSq = p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy;\n        if (speedSq < 0.04) {\n          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n          p.vx += Math.cos(angle) * 0.25 * config.speed;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.25 * config.speed;\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限\n        const maxVelocity = 7.5 * config.speed;\n        if (speedSq > maxVelocity * maxVelocity) {\n          const currentSpeed = Math.sqrt(speedSq);\n          p.vx = (p.vx / currentSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / currentSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx;\n        p.y += p.vy;\n\n        // 壁バウンド\n        if (p.x < 0) {\n          p.x = 0;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        } else if (p.x > width) {\n          p.x = width;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        }\n        if (p.y < 0) {\n          p.y = 0;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        } else if (p.y > height) {\n          p.y = height;\n         ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        } else {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル間の結合線描画\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; particlesRef.current[j];\n          const cdx = p1.x - p2.x;\n          const cdy = p1.y - p2.y;\n          const distSq = cdx * cdx + cdy * cdy;\n\n          if (distSq < connectionDistSq) {\n            const opacity = (1 - distSq / connectionDistSq) * 0.38;\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);\n            ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n            ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;\n            ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n            ctx.stroke();\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // バースト（花火・クリック時）パーティクル処理\n      for (let b = burstsRef.current.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {\n        const bp = burstsRef.current[b];\n        bp.x += bp.vx;\n        bp.y += bp.vy;\n        bp.vx *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy += 0.08; // わずかな重力\n        bp.alpha -= bp.decay;\n\n        if (bp.alpha <= 0) {\n          burstsRef.current.splice(b, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(bp.x, bp.y, Math.max(0.5, bp.size * bp.alpha), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = bp.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, bp.alpha);\n\n        if (config.glowEnabled) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 10;\n          ctx.shadowColor = bp.color;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n      animFrameIdRef.current =   setIsFullscreen(true);\n      }).catch(() => {\n        // フルスクリーン非対応または拒否時\n      });\n    } else {\n      if (document.exitFullscreen) {\n        document.exitFullscreen().then(() => {\n          setIsFullscreen(false);\n        }).catch(() => {});\n      }\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // 設定リセット\n  const handleResetConfig = useCallback(() => {\n    setConfig(DEFAULT_CONFIG);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 画面中央花火トリガー\n  const handleTriggerBurst = useCallback(() => {\n    setBurstTriggerCount((prev) => prev + 1);\n  }, []);\n\n  // パネル開閉\n  const togglePanel = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsPanelOpen((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 統計更新\n  const handleUpdateStats = useCallback((newFps: number, count: number) => {\n    setFps(newFps);\n    setActiveParticles(count);\n  }, []);\n\n  const currentTheme = THEME_PALETTES[config.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      {/* メインCanvas */}\n      <ParticleCanvas\n        config={config}\n        onUpdateStats={handleUpdateStats}\n        triggerBurstCount={burstTriggerCount}\n      />\n\n      {/* UIオーバーレイ層 */}\n      <div className=\"ui-overlay\">\n        {/* propsのundefinedガードとデフォルト値フォールバックを追加","dependencies":["src/types.ts"],"action":"modify","existingCode":"import { FC, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport {\n  ParticleConfig,\n  Particle,\n  BurstParticle,\n  THEME_PALETTES,\n} from './types';\n\ninterface ParticleCanvasProps {\n  config: ParticleConfig;\n  onUpdateStats?: (fps: number, count: number) => void;\n  triggerBurstCount?: number;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas: FC<ParticleCanvasProps> = ({\n  config,\n  onUpdateStats,\n  triggerBurstCount = 0,\n}) => {\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n  const burstsRef = useRef<BurstParticle[]>([]);\n  const pointerRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; active: boolean }>({\n    x: -9999,\n    y: -9999,\n    active: false,\n  });\n\n  const animFrameIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const lastTimeRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n  const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const prevTriggerBurstCount = useRef<number>(triggerBurstCount);\n\n  // 指定テーマのランダム色を取得\n  const getRandomThemeColor = useCallback(() => {\n    const palette = THEME_PALETTES[config.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n    const colors = palette.colors;\n    return colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];\n  }, [config.theme]);\n\n  // パーティクル1粒を初期化\n  const createParticle = useCallback(\n    (width: number, height: number): Particle => {\n      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const speed = (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.8) * config.speed;\n      const baseRadius = (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.9) * config.size;\n\n      return {\n        x: Math.random() * width,\n        y: Math.random() * height,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        baseRadius,\n        radius: baseRadius,\n        color: getRandomThemeColor(),\n        alpha: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.6,\n        life: Math.random() * 100,\n        maxLife: 150 + Math.random() * 100,\n      };\n    },\n    [config.speed, config.size, getRandomThemeColor]\n  );\n\n  // 花火・バーストエフェクトの発生\n  const addBurst = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number, burstAmount = 45) => {\n      const newBursts: BurstParticle[] = [];\n      const palette = THEME_PALETTES[config.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < burstAmount; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / burstAmount + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const velocity = 2.5 + Math.random() * 6.5;\n        const color = palette.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.colors.length)];\n\n        newBursts.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocity,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocity,\n          color,\n          alpha: 1.0,\n          size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.5,\n          decay: 0.012 + Math.random() * 0.018,\n        });\n      }\n      burstsRef.current.push(...newBursts);\n    },\n    [config.theme]\n  );\n\n  // パーティクル総数の調整\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const width = canvas.width;\n    const height = canvas.height;\n\n    const currentLength = particlesRef.current.length;\n    if (currentLength < config.count) {\n      for (let i = 0; i < config.count - currentLength; i++) {\n        particlesRef.current.push(createParticle(width, height));\n      }\n    } else if (currentLength > config.count) {\n      particlesRef.current.splice(config.count);\n    }\n  }, [config.count, createParticle]);\n\n  // テーマ変更時に色を再割り当て\n  useEffect(() => {\n    particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n      p.color = getRandomThemeColor();\n    });\n  }, [config.theme, getRandomThemeColor]);\n\n  // 外部からのバーストトリガー\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (triggerBurstCount > prevTriggerBurstCount.current) {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (canvas) {\n        const cx = canvas.width / 2;\n        const cy = canvas.height / 2;\n        addBurst(cx, cy, 70);\n      }\n      prevTriggerBurstCount.current = triggerBurstCount;\n    }\n  }, [triggerBurstCount, addBurst]);\n\n  // アニメーションループ & 描画\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    let isRunning = true;\n\n    // リサイズハンドラ\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      const displayWidth = canvas.clientWidth;\n      const displayHeight = canvas.clientHeight;\n\n      if (canvas.width !== displayWidth || canvas.height !== displayHeight) {\n        canvas.width = displayWidth;\n        canvas.height = displayHeight;\n\n        // 不足分を補充\n        while (particlesRef.current.length < config.count) {\n          particlesRef.current.push(createParticle(displayWidth, displayHeight));\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    handleResize();\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    const render = (time: number) => {\n      if (!isRunning) return;\n\n      const width = canvas.width;\n      const height = canvas.height;\n\n      // FPS計算（約1秒毎に更新）\n      frameCountRef.current++;\n      if (time - lastTimeRef.current >= 600) {\n        const fps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - lastTimeRef.current));\n        onUpdateStats?.(fps, particlesRef.current.length + burstsRef.current.length);\n        lastTimeRef.current = time;\n        frameCountRef.current = 0;\n      }\n\n      // 背景クリア（残像を残すために半透明黒でフェード）\n      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6, 8, 14, 0.28)';\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      const pointer = pointerRef.current;\n      const connectionDistSq = config.connectionDistance * config.connectionDistance;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadius = 220;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadiusSq = mouseInfluenceRadius * mouseInfluenceRadius;\n\n      // パーティクル物理演算\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // ポインターとの距離\n        const dx = pointer.x - p.x;\n        const dy = pointer.y - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n\n        if (pointer.active && distSq < mouseInfluenceRadiusSq && distSq > 4) {\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n          const force = (1 - dist / mouseInfluenceRadius);\n\n          if (config.mode === 'attract') {\n            // 引き寄せ\n            const pull = force * 0.6 * config.speed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull;\n          } else if (config.mode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            const push = force * 1.8 * config.speed;\n            p.vx -= (dx / dist) * push;\n            p.vy -= (dy / dist) * push;\n          } else if (config.mode === 'orbit') {\n            // 公転運動（法線ベクトル方向）\n            const normalX = -dy / dist;\n            const normalY = dx / dist;\n            const orbitSpeed = force * 1.5 * config.speed;\n            p.vx += normalX * orbitSpeed + (dx / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n            p.vy += normalY * orbitSpeed + (dy / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n          } else if (config.mode === 'trail') {\n            // 軌跡として追従\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force * 1.2 * config.speed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force * 1.2 * config.speed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 自然な空気抵抗\n        p.vx *= 0.96;\n        p.vy *= 0.96;\n\n        // 最低速度の維持（完全に止まらないように）\n        const speedSq = p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy;\n        if (speedSq < 0.04) {\n          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n          p.vx += Math.cos(angle) * 0.25 * config.speed;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.25 * config.speed;\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限\n        const maxVelocity = 7.5 * config.speed;\n        if (speedSq > maxVelocity * maxVelocity) {\n          const currentSpeed = Math.sqrt(speedSq);\n          p.vx = (p.vx / currentSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / currentSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx;\n        p.y += p.vy;\n\n        // 壁バウンド\n        if (p.x < 0) {\n          p.x = 0;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        } else if (p.x > width) {\n          p.x = width;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        }\n        if (p.y < 0) {\n          p.y = 0;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        } else if (p.y > height) {\n          p.y = height;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        }\n\n        // 呼吸パルス（サイズゆらぎ）\n        p.life += 0.035;\n        p.radius = p.baseRadius * (1 + Math.sin(p.life) * 0.28);\n\n        // 描画\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, p.radius), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n\n        if (config.glowEnabled) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 8;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        } else {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル間の結合線描画\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p1 = particlesRef.current[i];\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < particlesRef.current.length; j++) {\n          const p2 = particlesRef.current[j];\n          const cdx = p1.x - p2.x;\n          const cdy = p1.y - p2.y;\n          const distSq = cdx * cdx + cdy * cdy;\n\n          if (distSq < connectionDistSq) {\n            const opacity = (1 - distSq / connectionDistSq) * 0.38;\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);\n            ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n            ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;\n            ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n            ctx.stroke();\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // バースト（花火・クリック時）パーティクル処理\n      for (let b = burstsRef.current.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {\n        const bp = burstsRef.current[b];\n        bp.x += bp.vx;\n        bp.y += bp.vy;\n        bp.vx *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy += 0.08; // わずかな重力\n        bp.alpha -= bp.decay;\n\n        if (bp.alpha <= 0) {\n          burstsRef.current.splice(b, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(bp.x, bp.y, Math.max(0.5, bp.size * bp.alpha), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = bp.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, bp.alpha);\n\n        if (config.glowEnabled) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 10;\n          ctx.shadowColor = bp.color;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n      animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      isRunning = false;\n      if (animFrameIdRef.current) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);\n      }\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    };\n  }, [config, onUpdateStats, createParticle]);\n\n  // ポインターイベントリスナー\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n\n    pointerRef.current = { x, y, active: true };\n    addBurst(x, y, 40);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    pointerRef.current.x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    pointerRef.current.y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n    pointerRef.current.active = true;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    // 指を離しても位置は保ちつつ弱める\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n    pointerRef.current.x = -9999;\n    pointerRef.current.y = -9999;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"canvas-container\">\n      <canvas\n        ref={canvasRef}\n        className=\"particle-canvas\"\n        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n        onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}\n DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const sizeParam = safeConfig.size ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.size;\n      const speed = (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.8) * speedParam;\n      const baseRadius = (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.9) * sizeParam;\n\n      return {\n        x: Math.random() * width,\n        y: Math.random() * height,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        baseRadius,\n        radius: baseRadius,\n        color: getRandomThemeColor(),\n        alpha: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.6,\n        life: Math.random() * 100,\n        maxLife: 150 + Math.random() * 100,\n      };\n    },\n    [safeConfig.speed, safeConfig.size, getRandomThemeColor]\n  );\n\n  // 花火・バーストエフェクトの発生\n  const addBurst = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number, burstAmount = 45) => {\n      const newBursts: BurstParticle[] = [];\n      const themeKey = safeConfig.theme || DEFAULT_CONFIG.theme;\n      const palette = THEME_PALETTES[themeKey] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < burstAmount; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / burstAmount + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const velocity = 2.5 + Math.random() * 6.5;\n        const color = palette.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.colors.length)];\n\n        newBursts.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocity,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocity,\n          color,\n          alpha: 1.0,\n          size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.5,\n          decay: 0.012 + Math.random() * 0.018,\n        });\n      }\n      burstsRef.current.push(...newBursts);\n    },\n    [safeConfig.theme]\n  );\n\n  // パーティクル総数の調整\n  const targetCount = safeConfig.count ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.connectionDistance;\n      const connectionDistSq = connDistance * connDistance;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadius = 220;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadiusSq = mouseInfluenceRadius * mouseInfluenceRadius;\n      const currentSpeed = safeConfig.speed ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const currentMode = safeConfig.mode ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.mode;\n      const isGlow = safeConfig.glowEnabled ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.glowEnabled;\n\n      // パーティクル物理演算\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // ポインターとの距離\n        const dx = pointer.x - p.x;\n        const dy = pointer.y - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n\n        if (pointer.active && distSq < mouseInfluenceRadiusSq && distSq > 4) {\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n          const force = (1 - dist / mouseInfluenceRadius);\n\n          if (currentMode === 'attract') {\n            // 引き寄せ\n            const pull = force * 0.6 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            const push = force * 1.8 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx -= (dx / dist) * push;\n            p.vy -= (dy / dist) * push;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'orbit') {\n            // 公転運動（法線ベクトル方向）\n            const normalX = -dy / dist;\n            const normalY = dx / dist;\n            const orbitSpeed = force * 1.5 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += normalX * orbitSpeed + (dx / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n            p.vy += normalY * orbitSpeed + (dy / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n          } else if (currentMode === 'trail') {\n            // 軌跡として追従\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 自然な空気抵抗\n        p.vx *= 0.96;\n        p.vy *= 0.96;\n\n        // * currentSpeed;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.25 * currentSpeed;\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限\n        const maxVelocity = 7.5 * currentSpeed;\n        if (speedSq > maxVelocity * maxVelocity) {\n          const curSpeed = Math.sqrt(speedSq);\n          p.vx = (p.vx / curSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / curSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル間の結合線描画\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p1 = particlesRef.current[i];\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < particlesRef.current.length; j++) {\n          const p2 = particlesRef.current[j];\n          const cdx = p1.x - p2.x;\n          const cdy = p1.y - p2.y;\n          const distSq = cdx * cdx + cdy * cdy;\n\n          if (distSq < connectionDistSq) {\n            const opacity = (1 - distSq / connectionDistSq) * 0.38;\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);\n            ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n            ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;\n            ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n            ctx.stroke();\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // バースト（花火・クリック時）パーティクル処理\n      for (let b = burstsRef.current.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {\n        const bp = burstsRef.current[b];\n        bp.x += bp.vx;\n        bp.y += bp.vy;\n        bp.vx *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy += 0.08; // わずかな重力\n        bp.alpha -= bp.decay;\n\n        if        </button>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* パーティクル数 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <div  {/* 発光＆光線効果 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <label className=\"toggle-switch-label\">\n            <span>光のグローエフェクト</span>\n            <input\n </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ControlPanel;","language":"tsx"},{"path":"src/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import { FC, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport {\n  ParticleConfig,\n  Particle,\n  BurstParticle,\n  THEME_PALETTES,\n  DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n} from './types';\n\ninterface ParticleCanvasProps {\n  config?: ParticleConfig;\n => {\n  // config 未指定や未定義プロパティに対する完全フォールバック\n  const safeConfig: ParticleConfig = {\n    ...DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n    ...(config || {}),\n  };\n\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n  const burstsRef = useRef<BurstParticle[]>([]);\n  const pointerRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; active: boolean }>({\n    x: -9999,\n    y: -9999,\n    active: false,\n  });\n\n  const animFrameIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const lastTimeRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n  const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const prevTriggerBurstCount = useRef<number>(triggerBurstCount);\n\n  // 指定テーマのランダム色を取得\n  const getRandomThemeColor = useCallback(() => {\n    const themeKey = safeConfig.theme || DEFAULT_CONFIG.theme;\n    const palette = THEME_PALETTES[themeKey] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n    const colors = palette.colors;\n    return colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];\n  }, [safeConfig.theme]);\n\n  // パーティクル1粒を初期化\n  const createParticle = useCallback(\n    (width: number, height: number): Particle => {\n      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const speedParam = safeConfig.speed ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const sizeParam = safeConfig.size ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.size;\n      const speed = (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.8) * speedParam;\n      const baseRadius = (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.9) * sizeParam;\n\n      return {\n        x: Math.random() * width,\n        y: Math.random() * height,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        baseRadius,\n        radius: baseRadius,\n        color: getRandomThemeColor(),\n        alpha: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.6,\n        life: Math.random() * 100,\n        maxLife: 150 + Math.random() * 100,\n      };\n    },\n    [safeConfig.speed, safeConfig.size, getRandomThemeColor]\n  );\n\n  // 花火・バーストエフェクトの発生\n  const addBurst = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number, burstAmount = 45) => {\n      const newBursts: BurstParticle[] = [];\n      const themeKey = safeConfig.theme || DEFAULT_CONFIG.theme;\n      const palette = THEME_PALETTES[themeKey] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < burstAmount; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / burstAmount + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const velocity = 2.5 + Math.random() * 6.5;\n        const color = palette.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.colors.length)];\n\n        newBursts.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocity,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocity,\n          color,\n          alpha: 1.0,\n          size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.5,\n          decay: 0.012 + Math.random() * 0.018,\n        });\n      }\n      burstsRef.current.push(...newBursts);\n    },\n    [safeConfig.theme]\n  );\n\n  // パーティクル総数の調整\n  const targetCount = safeConfig.count ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.count;\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const width = canvas.width;\n    const height = canvas.height;\n\n    const currentLength = particlesRef.current.length;\n    if (currentLength < targetCount) {\n      for (let i = 0; i < targetCount - currentLength; i++) {\n        particlesRef.current.push(createParticle(width, height));\n      }\n    } else if (currentLength > targetCount) {\n      particlesRef.current.splice(targetCount);\n    }\n  }, [targetCount, createParticle]);\n\n  // テーマ変更時に色を再割り当て\n  useEffect(() => {\n    particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n      p.color = getRandomThemeColor();\n    });\n  }, [safeConfig.theme, getRandomThemeColor]);\n\n  // 外部からのバーストトリガー\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (triggerBurstCount > prevTriggerBurstCount.current) {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (canvas) {\n        const cx = canvas.width / 2;\n        const cy = canvas.height / 2;\n        addBurst(cx, cy, 70);\n      }\n      prevTriggerBurstCount.current = triggerBurstCount;\n    }\n  }, [triggerBurstCount, addBurst]);\n\n  // アニメーションループ & 描画\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    let isRunning = true;\n\n    // リサイズハンドラ\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const displayWidth = canvas.clientWidth;\n      const displayHeight = canvas.clientHeight;\n\n      if (canvas.width !== displayWidth || canvas.height !== displayHeight) {\n        canvas.width = displayWidth;\n        canvas.height = displayHeight;\n\n        // 不足分を補充\n        while (particlesRef.current.length < targetCount) {\n          particlesRef.current.push(createParticle(displayWidth, displayHeight));\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    handleResize();\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    const render = (time: number) => {\n      if (!isRunning) return;\n\n      const width = canvas.width;\n      const height = canvas.height;\n\n      // FPS計算（約0.6秒毎に更新）\n      frameCountRef.current++;\n      if (time - lastTimeRef.current >= 600) {\n        const fps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - lastTimeRef.current));\n        onUpdateStats?.(fps, particlesRef.current.length + burstsRef.current.length);\n        lastTimeRef.current = time;\n        frameCountRef.current = 0;\n      }\n\n      // 背景クリア（残像を残すために半透明黒でフェード）\n      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6, 8, 14, 0.28)';\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      const pointer = pointerRef.current;\n      const connDistance = safeConfig.connectionDistance ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.connectionDistance;\n      const connectionDistSq = connDistance * connDistance;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadius = 220;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadiusSq = mouseInfluenceRadius * mouseInfluenceRadius;\n      const currentSpeed = safeConfig.speed ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const currentMode = safeConfig.mode ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.mode;\n      const isGlow = safeConfig.glowEnabled ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.glowEnabled;\n\n      // パーティクル物理演算\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // ポインターとの距離\n        const dx = pointer.x - p.x;\n        const dy = pointer.y - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n\n        if (pointer.active && distSq < mouseInfluenceRadiusSq && distSq > 4) {\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n          const force = (1 - dist / mouseInfluenceRadius);\n\n          if (currentMode === 'attract') {\n            // 引き寄せ\n            const pull = force * 0.6 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            const push = force * 1.8 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx -= (dx / dist) * push;\n            p.vy -= (dy / dist) * push;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'orbit') {\n            // 公転運動（法線ベクトル方向）\n            const normalX = -dy / dist;\n            const normalY = dx / dist;\n            const orbitSpeed = force * 1.5 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += normalX * orbitSpeed + (dx / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n            p.vy += normalY * orbitSpeed + (dy / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n          } else if (currentMode === 'trail') {\n            // 軌跡として追従\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 自然な空気抵抗\n        p.vx *= 0.96;\n        p.vy *= 0.96;\n\n        // 最低速度の維持（完全に止まらないように）\n        const speedSq = p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy;\n        if (speedSq < 0.04) {\n          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n          p.vx += Math.cos(angle) * 0.25 * currentSpeed;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.25 * currentSpeed;\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限\n        const maxVelocity = 7.5 * currentSpeed;\n        if (speedSq > maxVelocity * maxVelocity) {\n          const curSpeed = Math.sqrt(speedSq);\n          p.vx = (p.vx / curSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / curSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx;\n        p.y += p.vy;\n\n        // 壁バウンド\n        if (p.x < 0) {\n          p.x = 0;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        } else if (p.x > width) {\n          p.x = width;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        }\n        if (p.y < 0) {\n          p.y = 0;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        } else if (p.y > height) {\n          p.y = height;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        }\n\n        // 呼吸パルス（サイズゆらぎ）\n        p.life += 0.035;\n        p.radius = p.baseRadius * (1 + Math.sin(p.life) * 0.28);\n\n        // 描画\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, p.radius), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n\n        if (isGlow) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 8;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        } else {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル間の結合線描画\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p1 = particlesRef.current[i];\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < particlesRef.current.length; j++) {\n          const p2 = particlesRef.current[j];\n          const cdx = p1.x - p2.x;\n          const cdy = p1.y - p2.y;\n          const distSq = cdx * cdx + cdy * cdy;\n\n          if (distSq < connectionDistSq) {\n            const opacity = (1 - distSq / connectionDistSq) * 0.38;\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);\n            ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n            ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;\n            ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n            ctx.stroke();\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // バースト（花火・クリック時）パーティクル処理\n      for (let b = burstsRef.current.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {\n        const bp = burstsRef.current[b];\n        bp.x += bp.vx;\n        bp.y += bp.vy;\n        bp.vx *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy += 0.08; // わずかな重力\n        bp.alpha -= bp.decay;\n\n        if (bp.alpha <= 0) {\n          burstsRef.current.splice(b, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(bp.x, bp.y, Math.max(0.5, bp.size * bp.alpha), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = bp.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, bp.alpha);\n\n        if (isGlow) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 10;\n          ctx.shadowColor = bp.color;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n      animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      isRunning = false;\n      if (animFrameIdRef.current) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);\n      }\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    };\n  }, [safeConfig, onUpdateStats, createParticle, targetCount]);\n\n  // ポインターイベントリスナー\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n\n    pointerRef.current = { x, y, active: true };\n    addBurst(x, y, 40);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    pointerRef.current.x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    pointerRef.current.y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n    pointerRef.current.active = true;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n    pointerRef.current.x = -9999;\n    pointerRef.current.y = -9999;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"canvas-container\">\n      <canvas\n        ref={canvasRef}\n        className=\"particle-canvas\"\n Activity,\n  Layers,\n  MousePointer,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport ParticleCanvas from './ParticleCanvas';\nimport ControlPanel from './ControlPanel';\nimport {\n  ParticleConfig,\n  DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n  THEME_PALETTES,\n} from './types';\nimport './index.css';\n\nconst App = () => {\n  // 初期値の安全なフォールバックを保証\n  const [config, setConfig] = useState<ParticleConfig>(() => ({\n    ...DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n  }));\n  const [isPanelOpen, setIsPanelOpen] = useState<boolean>(true);\n  const [fps, setFps] = useState<number>(60);\n  const [activeParticles, setActiveParticles] = useState<number>(\n    DEFAULT_CONFIG.count ?? 160\n  );\n  const [isFullscreen, setIsFullscreen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [burstTriggerCount, setBurstTriggerCount] = useState<number>(0);\n\n  // フルスクリーン切り替え\n  const toggleFullscreen = useCallback(() => {\n    if (!document.fullscreenElement) {\n      document.documentElement.requestFullscreen().then(() => {\n        setIsFullscreen(true);\n      }).catch(() => {\n        // フルスクリーン非対応または拒否時\n      });\n    } else {\n      if (document.exitFullscreen) {\n        document.exitFullscreen().then(() => {\n          setIsFullscreen(false);\n        }).catch(() => {});\n      }\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // 設定更新（部分的・不完全なオブジェクトが渡された場合も DEFAULT_CONFIG でマージ防御）\n  const handleConfigChange = useCallback((newConfig:  setConfig({ ...DEFAULT_CONFIG });\n  }, []);\n\n  // 画面中央花火トリガー\n  const handleTriggerBurst = useCallback(() => {\n    setBurstTriggerCount((prev) => prev + 1);\n  }, []);\n\n  // パネル開閉\n  const        <p className=\"header-subtitle\">\n              マウスや指を動かして光を操り、クリックで花火を咲かせよう\n            </p>\n          </div>\n\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '0.6rem' DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const sizeParam = safeConfig.size ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.size;\n      const speed = (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.8) * speedParam;\n      const baseRadius = (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.9) * sizeParam;\n\n      return {\n        x: Math.random() * width,\n        y: Math.random() * height,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        baseRadius,\n        radius: baseRadius,\n        color: getRandomThemeColor(),\n        alpha: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.6,\n        life: Math.random() * 100,\n        maxLife: 150 + Math.random() * 100,\n      };\n    },\n    [safeConfig.speed, safeConfig.size, getRandomThemeColor]\n  );\n\n  // 花火・バーストエフェクトの発生\n  const addBurst = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number, burstAmount = 45) => {\n      const newBursts: BurstParticle[] = [];\n      const themeKey = safeConfig.theme || DEFAULT_CONFIG.theme;\n      const palette = THEME_PALETTES[themeKey] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < burstAmount; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / burstAmount + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const velocity = 2.5 + Math.random() * 6.5;\n        const color = palette.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.colors.length)];\n\n        newBursts.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocity,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocity,\n          color,\n          alpha: 1.0,\n          size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.5,\n          decay: 0.012 + Math.random() * 0.018,\n        });\n      }\n      burstsRef.current.push(...newBursts);\n    },\n    [safeConfig.theme]\n  );\n\n  // パーティクル総数の調整\n  const targetCount = safeConfig.count ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.connectionDistance;\n      const connectionDistSq = connDistance * connDistance;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadius = 220;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadiusSq = mouseInfluenceRadius * mouseInfluenceRadius;\n      const currentSpeed = safeConfig.speed ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const currentMode = safeConfig.mode ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.mode;\n      const isGlow = safeConfig.glowEnabled ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.glowEnabled;\n\n      // パーティクル物理演算\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // ポインターとの距離\n        const dx = pointer.x - p.x;\n        const dy = pointer.y - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n\n        if (pointer.active && distSq < mouseInfluenceRadiusSq && distSq > 4) {\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n          const force = (1 - dist / mouseInfluenceRadius);\n\n          if (currentMode === 'attract') {\n            // 引き寄せ\n            const pull = force * 0.6 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            const push = force * 1.8 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx -= (dx / dist) * push;\n            p.vy -= (dy / dist) * push;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'orbit') {\n            // 公転運動（法線ベクトル方向）\n            const normalX = -dy / dist;\n            const normalY = dx / dist;\n            const orbitSpeed = force * 1.5 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += normalX * orbitSpeed + (dx / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n            p.vy += normalY * orbitSpeed + (dy / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n          } else if (currentMode === 'trail') {\n            // 軌跡として追従\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 自然な空気抵抗\n        p.vx *= 0.96;\n        p.vy *= 0.96;\n\n        // 最低速度の維持（完全に止まらないように）\n        const speedSq = p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy;\n        if (speedSq < 0.04) {\n          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n          p.vx += Math.cos(angle) * 0.25 * currentSpeed;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.25 * currentSpeed;\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限\n        const maxVelocity = 7.5 * currentSpeed;\n        if (speedSq > maxVelocity * maxVelocity) {\n          const curSpeed = Math.sqrt(speedSq);\n          p.vx = (p.vx / curSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / curSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx;\n        p.y += p.vy;\n\n        // 壁バウンド\n        if (p.x < 0) {\n          p.x = 0;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        } else if (p.x > width) {\n          p.x = width;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        }\n        if (p.y < 0) {\n          p.y = 0;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        } else if (p.y > height) {\n          p.y = height;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        }\n\n        // 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n\n        if (isGlow) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 8;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        } else {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル間の結合線描画\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p1 = particlesRef.current[i];\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < particlesRef.current.length; j++) {\n          const p2 = particlesRef.current[j];\n          const cdx = p1.x - p2.x;\n          const burstsRef.current[b];\n        bp.x += bp.vx;\n        bp.y += bp.vy;\n        bp.vx *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy += 0.08; // わずかな重力\n        bp.alpha -= bp.decay;\n\n        if (bp.alpha <= 0) {\n          burstsRef.current.splice(b, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(bp.x, bp.y, Math.max(0.5, bp.size * bp.alpha), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = bp.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, bp.alpha);\n\n        if (isGlow) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 10;\n          ctx.shadowColor = bp.color;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n      animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animFrameIdRef.current = pointerRef.current = { x, y, active: true };\n    addBurst(x, y, 40);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const sizeParam = safeConfig?.size ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.connectionDistance;\n      const connectionDistSq = connDistance * connDistance;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadius = 220;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadiusSq = mouseInfluenceRadius * mouseInfluenceRadius;\n      const currentSpeed = safeConfig?.speed ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const currentMode = safeConfig?.mode ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.mode;\n      const isGlow = safeConfig?.glowEnabled ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.glowEnabled;\n\n      // パーティクル物理演算\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // ポインターとの距離\n        const dx = pointer.x - p.x;\n        const dy = pointer.y - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n\n        if (pointer.active && distSq < mouseInfluenceRadiusSq && distSq > 4) {\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n          const force = (1 - dist / mouseInfluenceRadius);\n\n          if (currentMode === 'attract') {\n            // 引き寄せ\n            const pull = force * 0.6 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            const push = force * currentSpeed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 自然な空気抵抗\n        p.vx *= 0.96;\n        p.vy *= 0.96;\n\n        //          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル間の結合線描画\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);\n            ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n            ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;\n            ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n            ctx.stroke();\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // バースト（花火・クリック時）パーティクル処理\n      for (let b = burstsRef.current.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {\n        const bp = []);\n\n  const safeConfig: ParticleConfig = {\n    ...DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n    ...(config || {}),\n  };\n\n  const currentTheme = THEME_PALETTES[safeConfig.theme] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      {/* メインCanvas */}\n      <ParticleCanvas\n        config={safeConfig}\n        onUpdateStats={handleUpdateStats}\n        triggerBurstCount={burstTriggerCount}\n      />\n\n      {/* UIオーバーレイ層 */}\n      <div className=\"ui-overlay\">\n        {/* トップヘッダー */}\n        <header className=\"top-header\">\n          <div>\n            <h1 className=\"header-title\">\n              <Sparkles size={24} style={{ color: currentTheme?.colors?.[0] ?? '#38bdf8' }} />\n              <span>Lumina Particles</span>\n            </h1>\n            <p className=\"header-subtitle\">\n              マウスや指を動かして光を操り、クリックで花火を咲かせよう\n            </p>\n          </div>\n\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '0.6rem' }}>\n            <div className=\"stats-badge\" title=\"描画フレームレート\">\n              <Activity size={12} />\n              <span>{fps} FPS</span>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"stats-badge\" title=\"アクティブ粒子数\">\n              <Layers size={12} />\n              <span>{activeParticles} 個</span>\n            </div>\n            <button\n              className=\"icon-btn\"\n              onClick={toggleFullscreen}\n              title={isFullscreen ? 'フルスクリーン解除' : '全画面表示'}\n              aria-label=\"フルスクリーン切替\"\n            >\n              {isFullscreen ? <Minimize2 size={18} /> : <Maximize2 size={18} />}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </header>\n\n        {/* 下部インタラクションヒント */}\n        <div className=\"hint-overlay\">\n          <div className=\"hint-badge\">\n            <MousePointer size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-cyan)' }} />\n            <span>タップやドラッグで粒子が反応・クリックでスパーク炸裂</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 設定コントロールパネル */}\n      <ControlPanel\n        isOpen={isPanelOpen}\n        onToggle={togglePanel}\n        config={safeConfig}\n        onChangeConfig={handleConfigChange}\n        onReset={handleResetConfig}\n        onTriggerBurst={handleTriggerBurst}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"},{"path":"src/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import { FC, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport {\n  ParticleConfig,\n  Particle,\n  BurstParticle,\n  THEME_PALETTES,\n  DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n} from './types';\n\ninterface ParticleCanvasProps {\n  config?: ParticleConfig;\n  onUpdateStats?: (fps: number, count: number) => void;\n  triggerBurstCount?: number;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas: FC<ParticleCanvasProps> = ({\n  config,\n  onUpdateStats,\n  triggerBurstCount = 0,\n}) => {\n  // config 未指定や未定義プロパティに対する完全フォールバック\n  const safeConfig: ParticleConfig = {\n    ...DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n    ...(config || {}),\n  };\n\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n  const burstsRef = useRef<BurstParticle[]>([]);\n  const pointerRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; active: boolean }>({\n    x: -9999,\n    y: -9999,\n    active: false,\n  });\n\n  const animFrameIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const lastTimeRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n  const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const prevTriggerBurstCount = useRef<number>(triggerBurstCount);\n\n  // 指定テーマのランダム色を取得\n  const getRandomThemeColor = useCallback(() => {\n    const themeKey = safeConfig?.theme || DEFAULT_CONFIG.theme;\n    const palette = THEME_PALETTES[themeKey] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n    const colors = palette?.colors || THEME_PALETTES.aurora.colors;\n    return colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];\n  }, [safeConfig?.theme]);\n\n  // パーティクル1粒を初期化\n  const createParticle = useCallback(\n    (width: number, height: number): Particle => {\n      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const speedParam = safeConfig?.speed ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const sizeParam = safeConfig?.size ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.size;\n      const speed = (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.8) * speedParam;\n      const baseRadius = (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.9) * sizeParam;\n\n      return {\n        x: Math.random() * width,\n        y: Math.random() * height,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        baseRadius,\n        radius: baseRadius,\n        color: getRandomThemeColor(),\n        alpha: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.6,\n        life: Math.random() * 100,\n        maxLife: 150 + Math.random() * 100,\n      };\n    },\n    [safeConfig?.speed, safeConfig?.size, getRandomThemeColor]\n  );\n\n  // 花火・バーストエフェクトの発生\n  const addBurst = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number, burstAmount = 45) => {\n      const newBursts: BurstParticle[] = [];\n      const themeKey = safeConfig?.theme || DEFAULT_CONFIG.theme;\n      const palette = THEME_PALETTES[themeKey] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n      const colors = palette?.colors || THEME_PALETTES.aurora.colors;\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < burstAmount; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / burstAmount + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const velocity = 2.5 + Math.random() * 6.5;\n        const color = colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];\n\n        newBursts.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocity,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocity,\n          color,\n          alpha: 1.0,\n          size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.5,\n          decay: 0.012 + Math.random() * 0.018,\n        });\n      }\n      burstsRef.current.push(...newBursts);\n    },\n    [safeConfig?.theme]\n  );\n\n  // パーティクル総数の調整\n  const targetCount = safeConfig?.count ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.count;\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const width = canvas.width;\n    const height = canvas.height;\n\n    const currentLength = particlesRef.current.length;\n    if (currentLength < targetCount) {\n      for (let i = 0; i < targetCount - currentLength; i++) {\n        particlesRef.current.push(createParticle(width, height));\n      }\n    } else if (currentLength > targetCount) {\n      particlesRef.current.splice(targetCount);\n    }\n  }, [targetCount, createParticle]);\n\n  // テーマ変更時に色を再割り当て\n  useEffect(() => {\n    particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n      p.color = getRandomThemeColor();\n    });\n  }, [safeConfig?.theme, getRandomThemeColor]);\n\n  // 外部からのバーストトリガー\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (triggerBurstCount > prevTriggerBurstCount.current) {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (canvas) {\n        const cx = canvas.width / 2;\n        const cy = canvas.height / 2;\n        addBurst(cx, cy, 70);\n      }\n      prevTriggerBurstCount.current = triggerBurstCount;\n    }\n  }, [triggerBurstCount, addBurst]);\n\n  // アニメーションループ & 描画\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    let isRunning = true;\n\n    // リサイズハンドラ\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const displayWidth = canvas.clientWidth;\n      const displayHeight = canvas.clientHeight;\n\n      if (canvas.width !== displayWidth || canvas.height !== displayHeight) {\n        canvas.width = displayWidth;\n        canvas.height = displayHeight;\n\n        // 不足分を補充\n        while (particlesRef.current.length < targetCount) {\n          particlesRef.current.push(createParticle(displayWidth, displayHeight));\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    handleResize();\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    const render = (time: number) => {\n      if (!isRunning) return;\n\n      const width = canvas.width;\n      const height = canvas.height;\n\n      // FPS計算（約0.6秒毎に更新）\n      frameCountRef.current++;\n      if (time - lastTimeRef.current >= 600) {\n        const fps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - lastTimeRef.current));\n        onUpdateStats?.(fps, particlesRef.current.length + burstsRef.current.length);\n        lastTimeRef.current = time;\n        frameCountRef.current = 0;\n      }\n\n      // 背景クリア（残像を残すために半透明黒でフェード）\n      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6, 8, 14, 0.28)';\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      const pointer = pointerRef.current;\n      const connDistance = safeConfig?.connectionDistance ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.connectionDistance;\n      const connectionDistSq = connDistance * connDistance;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadius = 220;\n      const mouseInfluenceRadiusSq = mouseInfluenceRadius * mouseInfluenceRadius;\n      const currentSpeed = safeConfig?.speed ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n      const currentMode = safeConfig?.mode ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.mode;\n      const isGlow = safeConfig?.glowEnabled ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.glowEnabled;\n\n      // パーティクル物理演算\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // ポインターとの距離\n        const dx = pointer.x - p.x;\n        const dy = pointer.y - p.y;\n        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n\n        if (pointer.active && distSq < mouseInfluenceRadiusSq && distSq > 4) {\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);\n          const force = (1 - dist / mouseInfluenceRadius);\n\n          if (currentMode === 'attract') {\n            // 引き寄せ\n            const pull = force * 0.6 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pull;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pull;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            const push = force * 1.8 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx -= (dx / dist) * push;\n            p.vy -= (dy / dist) * push;\n          } else if (currentMode === 'orbit') {\n            // 公転運動（法線ベクトル方向）\n            const normalX = -dy / dist;\n            const normalY = dx / dist;\n            const orbitSpeed = force * 1.5 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vx += normalX * orbitSpeed + (dx / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n            p.vy += normalY * orbitSpeed + (dy / dist) * (force * 0.15);\n          } else if (currentMode === 'trail') {\n            // 軌跡として追従\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force * 1.2 * currentSpeed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 自然な空気抵抗\n        p.vx *= 0.96;\n        p.vy *= 0.96;\n\n        // 最低速度の維持（完全に止まらないように）\n        const speedSq = p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy;\n        if (speedSq < 0.04) {\n          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n          p.vx += Math.cos(angle) * 0.25 * currentSpeed;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.25 * currentSpeed;\n        }\n\n        // 速度上限\n        const maxVelocity = 7.5 * currentSpeed;\n        if (speedSq > maxVelocity * maxVelocity) {\n          const curSpeed = Math.sqrt(speedSq);\n          p.vx = (p.vx / curSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / curSpeed) * maxVelocity;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx;\n        p.y += p.vy;\n\n        // 壁バウンド\n        if (p.x < 0) {\n          p.x = 0;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        } else if (p.x > width) {\n          p.x = width;\n          p.vx *= -1;\n        }\n        if (p.y < 0) {\n          p.y = 0;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        } else if (p.y > height) {\n          p.y = height;\n          p.vy *= -1;\n        }\n\n        // 呼吸パルス（サイズゆらぎ）\n        p.life += 0.035;\n        p.radius = p.baseRadius * (1 + Math.sin(p.life) * 0.28);\n\n        // 描画\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, p.radius), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n\n        if (isGlow) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 8;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        } else {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル間の結合線描画\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p1 = particlesRef.current[i];\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < particlesRef.current.length; j++) {\n          const p2 = particlesRef.current[j];\n          const cdx = p1.x - p2.x;\n          const cdy = p1.y - p2.y;\n          const distSq = cdx * cdx + cdy * cdy;\n\n          if (distSq < connectionDistSq) {\n            const opacity = (1 - distSq / connectionDistSq) * 0.38;\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);\n            ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n            ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;\n            ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n            ctx.stroke();\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // バースト（花火・クリック時）パーティクル処理\n      for (let b = burstsRef.current.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {\n        const bp = burstsRef.current[b];\n        bp.x += bp.vx;\n        bp.y += bp.vy;\n        bp.vx *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy *= 0.94;\n        bp.vy += 0.08; // わずかな重力\n        bp.alpha -= bp.decay;\n\n        if (bp.alpha <= 0) {\n          burstsRef.current.splice(b, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(bp.x, bp.y, Math.max(0.5, bp.size * bp.alpha), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = bp.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, bp.alpha);\n\n        if (isGlow) {\n          ctx.shadowBlur = 10;\n          ctx.shadowColor = bp.color;\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;\n      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n\n      animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      isRunning = false;\n      if (animFrameIdRef.current) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);\n      }\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    };\n  }, [safeConfig, onUpdateStats, createParticle, targetCount]);\n\n  // ポインターイベントリスナー\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n\n    pointerRef.current = { x, y, active: true };\n    addBurst(x, y, 40);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    pointerRef.current.x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    pointerRef.current.y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n    pointerRef.current.active = true;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n    pointerRef.current.x = -9999;\n    pointerRef.current.y = -9999;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"canvas-container\">\n      <canvas\n        ref={canvasRef}\n        className=\"particle-canvas\"\n        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n        onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}\n        onPointerLeave={handlePointerLeave}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ParticleCanvas;","language":"tsx"},{"path":"src/ControlPanel.tsx","content":"import { FC, ChangeEvent } from 'react';\nimport {\n  Sliders,\n  RotateCcw,\n  Sparkles,\n  Magnet,\n  Zap,\n  Orbit,\n  MousePointer2,\n  X,\n  Palette\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport {\n  ParticleConfig,\n  ThemeType,\n  ParticleMode,\n  THEME_PALETTES,\n  DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n} from './types';\n\ninterface ControlPanelProps {\n  isOpen: boolean;\n  onToggle: () => void;\n  config?: ParticleConfig;\n  onChangeConfig: (newConfig: ParticleConfig) => void;\n  onReset: () => void;\n  onTriggerBurst: () => void;\n}\n\nconst ControlPanel: FC<ControlPanelProps> = ({\n  isOpen,\n  onToggle,\n  config,\n  onChangeConfig,\n  onReset,\n  onTriggerBurst,\n}) => {\n  // config の安全なフォールバックを保証（undefined の count 参照エラー防止）\n  const safeConfig: ParticleConfig = {\n    ...DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n    ...(config || {}),\n  };\n\n  const handleThemeChange = (theme: ThemeType) => {\n    onChangeConfig({ ...safeConfig, theme });\n  };\n\n  const handleModeChange = (mode: ParticleMode) => {\n    onChangeConfig({ ...safeConfig, mode });\n  };\n\n  const handleSliderChange = (\n    key: keyof Pick<ParticleConfig, 'count' | 'speed' | 'size' | 'connectionDistance'>,\n    e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>\n  ) => {\n    const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);\n    onChangeConfig({\n      ...safeConfig,\n      [key]: isNaN(val) ? (DEFAULT_CONFIG[key] ?? 100) : val,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handleToggleGlow = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    onChangeConfig({\n      ...safeConfig,\n      glowEnabled: e.target.checked,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const modeDescriptions: Record<ParticleMode, { label: string; icon: JSX.Element }> = {\n    attract: { label: '引き寄せ', icon: <Magnet size={16} /> },\n    repel: { label: '反発', icon: <Zap size={16} /> },\n    trail: { label: '軌跡追従', icon: <MousePointer2 size={16} /> },\n    orbit: { label: '公転回転', icon: <Orbit size={16} /> },\n  };\n\n  const themes: ThemeType[] = ['aurora', 'cosmic', 'sakura', 'neon', 'gold'];\n\n  const countValue = safeConfig?.count ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.count;\n  const speedValue = safeConfig?.speed ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.speed;\n  const sizeValue = safeConfig?.size ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.size;\n  const distanceValue = safeConfig?.connectionDistance ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.connectionDistance;\n  const glowValue = safeConfig?.glowEnabled ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.glowEnabled;\n  const currentTheme = safeConfig?.theme ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.theme;\n  const currentMode = safeConfig?.mode ?? DEFAULT_CONFIG.mode;\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {!isOpen && (\n        <button\n          className=\"toggle-panel-btn\"\n          onClick={onToggle}\n          title=\"設定パネルを開く\"\n          aria-label=\"設定パネルを開く\"\n        >\n          <Sliders size={20} />\n        </button>\n      )}\n\n      <div className={isOpen ? 'control-panel' : 'control-panel panel-collapsed'}>\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '0.5rem', fontWeight: 600 }}>\n            <Sliders size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-cyan)' }} />\n            <span>エフェクト設定</span>\n          </div>\n          <button\n            className=\"icon-btn\"\n            style={{ padding: '0.35rem' }}\n            onClick={onToggle}\n            title=\"閉じる\"\n            aria-label=\"設定パネルを閉じる\"\n          >\n            <X size={16} />\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* カラーテーマ選択 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <label className=\"control-label\">\n            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '0.35rem' }}>\n              <Palette size={14} /> カラーテーマ\n            </span>\n          </label>\n          <div className=\"theme-button-grid\">\n            {themes.map((t) => {\n              const pal = THEME_PALETTES[t] || THEME_PALETTES.aurora;\n              const isActive = currentTheme === t;\n              return (\n                <button\n                  key={t}\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  className={isActive ?          </button>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* パーティクル数 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <div className=\"control-label\">\n            <span>パーティクル数</span>\n            <span className=\"slider-value\">{countValue}</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"slider-container\">\n            <input\n              type=\"range\"\n              min=\"50\"\n              max=\"350\"\n              step=\"10\"\n              value={countValue}\n              onChange={(e) => handleSliderChange('count', e)}\n              className=\"slider-input\"\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 移動速度 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <div className=\"control-label\">\n            <span>移動速度</span>\n            <span className=\"slider-value\">{speedValue.toFixed(1)}x</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"slider-container\">\n            <input\n              type=\"range\"\n              min=\"0.4\"\n              max=\"4.0\"\n              step=\"0.2\"\n              value={speedValue}\n              onChange={(e) => handleSliderChange('speed', e)}\n              className=\"slider-input\"\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 粒子の大きさ */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <div className=\"control-label\">\n            <span>粒子の大きさ</span>\n            <span className=\"slider-value\">{sizeValue.toFixed(1)}px</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"slider-container\">\n            <input\n              type=\"range\"\n              min=\"1.0\"\n              max=\"5.0\"\n              step=\"0.2\"\n              value={sizeValue}\n              onChange={(e) => handleSliderChange('size', e)}\n              className=\"slider-input\"\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 接続距離 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <div className=\"control-label\">\n            <span>接続線の距離</span>\n            <span className=\"slider-value\">{distanceValue}px</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"slider-container\">\n            <input\n              type=\"range\"\n              min=\"40\"\n              max=\"200\"\n              step=\"5\"\n              value={distanceValue}\n              onChange={(e) => handleSliderChange('connectionDistance', e)}\n              className=\"slider-input\"\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 発光＆光線効果 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <label className=\"toggle-switch-label\">\n            <span>光のグローエフェクト</span>\n            <input\n
